It keeps accounts, categories, and cash\ntransactions in a relational database reached only through the shipped persistence library, rejects\na failing command before it writes, enforces non-negative balances, and reports balances, category\ntotals, and monthly totals. All values come from queries; no report row, count, or total is embedded.\n\n## Technology Stack\n\nDerived from `TECHNOLOGY_STACK.md`, which remains the decision of record.\n\n| Technology | Where it applies |\n|---|---|\n| Python 3.11 or newer | Implementation language for every module. |\n| SQLite | The database, reached only through the shipped persistence package. |\n| CLI | The `ledger` executable at the application root and the `cli` package behind it. |\n| pytest | The test runner behind `bin/test.sh`. |\n| POSIX sh | `bin/test.sh` and the supplied grader `sources/run.sh`. |\n\n## Project Layout\n\n| Path | Role |\n|---|---|\n| `ledger` | Executable at the application root; calls `cli.main` and exits with its return value. |\n| `cli/` | Command-line package: dispatch, errors, validators, money, output, commands. |\n| `cli/commands/` | One module per command; each registers a handler with the command registry. |\n| `persistence/` | Generated and shipped by Drydock from `DATABASE.md`; never hand-written. |\n| `tests/` | The project's own pytest suite, with `conftest.py` exposing `db_session` and `cli_runner`. |\n| `bin/test.sh` | POSIX sh launcher: activates `.venv`, runs `pytest -q` from the root, exits zero only when all tests pass. |\n| `pyproject.toml`, `uv.lock` | Dependency manifest and committed lock; environment created by `uv venv`. |\n| `sources/` | Read-only scoring assets staged by Drydock; application code never reads them. |\n\n## Module Ownership\n\n| Boundary | Owning module | Allowed low-level access |\n|---|---|---|\n| Persistence | `persistence/` (generated) | The only package that imports a database driver. |\n| Configuration | `persistence.open_database` via `cli.database` | Resolves `DATABASE_URL`, then `DATABASE_PATH`, then `data/app.db`. |\n| File stores | None | Command modules read only the CSV path named on the command line. |\n| External services | None | No network access. |\n\nCallers use `db.<table>.<operation>(...)` as declared in `DATABASE.md`. SQL, sessions, table names,\nand column names never cross that boundary, and no command issues DDL.\n\n## Technical Decisions\n\n- Money is integer cents at every layer; floating point is never used. Parsing and formatting use\n  integer arithmetic only.\n- Every command validates its full input and every business rule before its first write.\n- Sums, counts, balances, and the next transaction id are computed by services from listed rows.\n- Standard output carries only CSV (`\\n` endings, no quoting); diagnostics go to standard error.\n- Exit codes: 0 success, 1 operational, 2 usage, 3 rejected, 4 not found.\n- Commands register themselves in a registry; the dispatcher checks arity before opening the database.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC architecture-python-version ===\nIntent: The build runs on Python 3.11 or newer, as the technology stack requires.\n\nimport sys\n\nassert sys.version_info >= (3, 11)\n=== END AC architecture-python-version ===\n\n=== AC bin/test.sh exists and is syntactically valid POSIX sh.\n\nimport subprocess\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\nlauncher = Path(\"bin/test.sh\")\nassert launcher.is_file()\nresult = subprocess.run([\"sh\", reads `sources/run.sh` or any seed file, and never embeds a report answer.\n- Nothing under `sources/` is modified.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-CliFoundation.md ===\n# FEATURE: CLI Foundation\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261025 V1 |\n| Description | The ledger executable, command dispatch, exit-code mapping, CSV output, and shared input validators. |\n| Depends On | DATABASE.md, ARCHITECTURE.md |\n| Provides | ledger, cli.main, cli.testing.cli_runner, cli.errors, cli.validate, cli.money, cli.output, cli.registry |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nProvide the executable and the shared machinery every command uses, so command stories write only\ntheir own behavior.\n\n## Interface\n\n| Module | Contract |\n|---|---|\n| `ledger` | Executable at the application root; runs `cli.main(sys.argv[1:])` and exits with the result. |\n| `cli/__init__.py` | `main(argv: list[str] \\| None = None) -> int`. |\n| `cli/testing.py` | `cli_runner(*args) -> (exit_code, stdout, stderr)` without spawning a process. |\n| `cli/errors.py` | `LedgerError` with an `exit_code` attribute; subclasses `Operational` (1), `Usage` (2), `Rejected` (3), `NotFound` (4). |\n| `cli/registry.py` | `register(path, min_args, max_args, handler)` and `COMMANDS`. A handler is `run(db, args) -> list[list[str]]` returning the header and data rows. |\n| `cli/commands/` | Every module is imported at startup; each registers its handlers. |\n| `cli/database.py` | `open_ledger()` opens the store with `open_database`, using `DATABASE_URL`, then `DATABASE_PATH`, then `data/app.db`. |\n| `cli/validate.py` | `validate_code`, `validate_name`, `validate_memo`, `validate_date`; each returns the value or raises `Rejected`. |\n| `cli/money.py` | `parse_amount(text) -> int` cents and `format_amount(cents) -> str`, integer arithmetic only. |\n| `cli/output.py` | `write_rows(rows)` writes comma-separated rows with `\\n` endings and no quoting; `TRANSACTION_HEADER`, `format_transaction(row)`. |\n\n## Behavior\n\n- The dispatcher resolves the command path and checks the argument count before opening the\n  database. An unknown command, unknown table or report, or wrong argument count exits 2.\n- A `LedgerError` is mapped to its exit code; the message goes to standard error as one line and\n  nothing is written to standard output. Any other database failure exits 1.\n- Output is written only after the handler returns, so a failing command writes nothing to\n  standard output.\n- Codes are 1 to 12 characters from `A-Z` and `0-9`. Names are 1 to 40 characters. Memos are 0 to\n  60 characters. None contains a comma or newline. Dates are real calendar dates `YYYY-MM-DD`.\n- Amounts match `-?DIGITS[.D[D]]`, never zero. Output amounts have exactly two decimals, a leading\n  `-` when negative, and no thousands separator.\n- A non-integer transaction id is rejected with exit 3 (disclosed decision Q-003).\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is owned by DATABASE.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC clifoundation-executable ===\nIntent: The ledger executable exists at the application root and is FEATURE-BalanceRules.md ===\n# FEATURE: Balance Rules\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261025 V1 |\n| Description | Account summaries and the non-negative balance check used by every mutating command. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-CliFoundation.md, DATABASE.md |\n| Provides | cli.balance.account_summary, cli.balance.balances, cli.balance.check_balances, cli.balance.next_txn_id |\n| Consumes | transactions.list, accounts.get |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nAn account's balance is the sum of its transaction amounts. No command may leave a balance below\nzero. This module computes balances from stored rows in integer cents and applies the rule before a\ncommand writes, so a violation changes nothing.\n\n## Interface\n\n| Function | Contract |\n|---|---|\n| `account_summary(db, code) -> tuple[int, int]` | Transaction count and balance in cents for the account, computed from its stored transactions; `(0, 0)` when it has none. |\n| `balances(db) -> dict[str, int]` | Balance in cents per account that has transactions. |\n| `check_balances(db, deltas)` | `deltas` maps account code to the net change in cents the command would make. Raises `Rejected` (exit 3) when any stored balance plus its delta is below zero; returns `None` otherwise. Writes nothing. |\n| `next_txn_id(db) -> int` | One more than the largest stored `txn_id`, or `1` when the table is empty. |\n\nCommands express a post as `{account: amount}`, an amend as `{account: new - old}`, a delete as\n`{account: -amount}`, a transfer as both sides, and a load as the per-account sum of the file.\nA balance of exactly zero is allowed. Computation is service-side from listed rows (decision Q-002).\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-TRANSACTIONS-003` — read: list transactions for balances and the largest id - supplies: `account` - returns: `txn_id`, `account`, `amount`\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNTS-002` — read: confirm an account exists - supplies: `account` - returns: `account`, `name`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC balancerules-summary ===\nIntent: account_summary returns the stored transaction count and the integer-cent sum of its amounts.\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom cli.balance import account_summary\n\nacct = \"UTBAL1\"\ncat = \"UTBALC1\"\namounts = [1000, -300, 250]\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=acct, name=\"Balance Probe\")\n    db.categories.create(category=cat, description=\"Balance Category\")\n    for offset, cents in enumerate(amounts):\n        db.transactions.create(\n            txn_id=980001 + offset, date=\"2026-04-01\", account=acct,\n            category=cat, amount=cents, memo=\"\",\n        )\n    count, balance = account_summary(db, acct)\n    assert count == len(amounts)\n    assert balance == sum(amounts)\n    assert isinstance(balance, int)\n=== END AC balancerules-summary ===\n\n=== AC balancerules-summary-empty ===\nIntent: An account with no transactions has a zero count and zero balance.\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom cli.balance import account_summary\n\nacct = \"UTBAL2\"\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=acct, name=\"Empty Probe\")\n    count, balance = account_summary(db, acct)\n    assert count == 0\n    assert balance == 0\n=== END AC balancerules-summary-empty ===\n\n=== AC balancerules-balances ===\nIntent: balances returns the per-account sums computed from stored rows.\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom cli.balance import balances\n\nacct_a = \"UTBAL3A\"\nacct_b = \"UTBAL3B\"\ncat = \"UTBALC3\"\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=acct_a, name=\"A\")\n    db.accounts.create(account=acct_b, name=\"B\")\n    db.categories.create(category=cat, description=\"Cat\")\n    plan = [(980011, acct_a, 500), (980012, acct_a, -200), (980013, acct_b, 75)]\n    for txn_id, acct, cents in plan:\n        db.transactions.create(\n            txn_id=txn_id, date=\"2026-04-02\", account=acct, category=cat, amount=cents, memo=\"\"\n        )\n    result = balances(db)\n    assert result[acct_a] == sum(c for _, a, c in plan if a == acct_a)\n    assert result[acct_b] == sum(c for _, a, c in plan if a == acct_b)\n=== END AC balancerules-balances ===\n\n=== AC balancerules-check-boundary ===\nIntent: check_balances accepts a change that lands exactly on zero and rejects one cent below.\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom cli.balance import check_balances\nfrom cli.errors import Rejected\n\nacct = \"UTBAL4\"\ncat = \"UTBALC4\"\ncents = 700\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=acct, name=\"Boundary\")\n    db.categories.create(category=cat, description=\"Cat\")\n    db.transactions.create(\n        txn_id=980021, date=\"2026-04-03\", account=acct, category=cat, amount=cents, memo=\"\"\n    )\n    assert check_balances(db, {acct: -cents}) is None\n    try:\n        check_balances(db, {acct: -cents - 1})\n    except Rejected as exc:\n        assert exc.exit_code == 3\n    else:\n        raise AssertionError(\"negative balance accepted\")\n=== END AC balancerules-check-boundary ===\n\n=== AC balancerules-check-any-account ===\nIntent: check_balances rejects when any one of several accounts would go negative.\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom cli.balance import db.accounts.create(account=poor, name=\"Poor\")\n    db.categories.create(category=cat, description=\"Cat\")\n    db.transactions.create(\n        txn_id=980031, date=\"2026-04-04\", account=rich, BEGIN ARTIFACT ARCHITECTURE.md ===\n# ARCHITECTURE: LayersTesting\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261025 V1 |\n| Description | Modules, boundaries, and technical decisions for the LayersTesting cash ledger CLI. |\n| Depends On | None |\n| Provides | bin/test.sh, pyproject.toml, tests/conftest.py |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ledger` is a single-executable command-line cash ledger. It keeps accounts, categories, and cash\ntransactions in a relational database reached only through the shipped persistence library, rejects\na failing command before it writes, enforces non-negative balances, and reports balances, category\ntotals, and monthly totals. All values come from queries; no report row, count, or total is embedded.\n\n## Technology Stack\n\nDerived from `TECHNOLOGY_STACK.md`, which remains the decision of record.\n\n| Technology | Where it applies |\n|---|---|\n| Python 3.11 or newer | Implementation language for every module. |\n| SQLite | The database, reached only through the shipped persistence package. |\n| CLI | The `ledger` executable at the application root and the `cli` package behind it. |\n| pytest | The test runner behind `bin/test.sh`. |\n| POSIX sh | `bin/test.sh` and the supplied grader `sources/run.sh`. |\n\n## Project Layout\n\n| Path | Role |\n|---|---|\n| `ledger` | Executable at the application root; calls `cli.main` and exits with its return value. |\n| `cli/` | Command-line package: dispatch, errors, validators, money, output, commands. |\n| `cli/commands/` | One module per command; each registers a handler with the command registry. |\n| `persistence/` | Generated and shipped by Drydock from `DATABASE.md`; never hand-written. |\n| `tests/` | The project's own pytest suite, with `conftest.py` exposing `db_session` and `cli_runner`. |\n| `bin/test.sh` | POSIX sh launcher: activates `.venv`, runs `pytest -q` from the root, exits zero only when all tests pass. |\n| `pyproject.toml`, `uv.lock` | Dependency manifest and committed lock; environment created by `uv venv`. |\n| `sources/` | Read-only scoring assets staged by Drydock; application code never reads them. |\n\n## Module Ownership\n\n| Boundary | Owning module | Allowed low-level access |\n|---|---|---|\n| Persistence | `persistence/` (generated) | The only package that imports a database driver. |\n| Configuration | `persistence.open_database` via `cli.database` | Resolves `DATABASE_URL`, then `DATABASE_PATH`, then `data/app.db`. |\n| File stores | None | Command modules read only the CSV path named on the command line. |\n| External services | None | No network access. |\n\nCallers use `db.<table>.<operation>(...)` as declared in `DATABASE.md`. SQL, sessions, table names,\nand column names never cross that boundary, and no command issues DDL.\n\n## Technical Decisions\n\n- Money is integer cents at every layer; floating point is never used. Parsing and formatting use\n  integer arithmetic only.\n- Every command validates its full input and every business rule before its first write.\n- Sums, counts, balances, and the next transaction id are computed by services from listed rows.\n- Standard output carries only CSV (`\\n` endings, no quoting); diagnostics go to standard error.\n- Exit codes: 0 success, 1 operational, 2 usage, 3 rejected, 4 not found.\n- Commands register themselves in a registry; the dispatcher checks arity before opening the database.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC architecture-python-version ===\nIntent: The build runs on Python 3.11 or newer, as the technology stack requires.\n\nimport sys\n\nassert sys.version_info >= (3, 11)\n=== END AC architecture-python-version ===\n\n=== AC architecture-pyproject ===\nIntent: pyproject.toml parses as TOML and declares a project table.\n\nimport tomllib\nfrom pathlib import Path\n\npath = Path(\"pyproject.toml\")\nassert path.is_file()\ndata = tomllib.loads(path.read_text(encoding=\"utf-8\"))\nassert \"project\" in data\n=== END AC architecture-pyproject ===\n\n=== AC architecture-test-launcher ===\nIntent: reads `sources/run.sh` or any seed file, and never embeds a report answer.\n- Nothing under `sources/` is modified.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n=== BEGIN ARTIFACT FEATURE-CliFoundation.md ===\n# FEATURE: CLI Foundation\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261025 V1 |\n| Description | The ledger executable, command dispatch, exit-code mapping, CSV output, and shared input validators. |\n| Depends On | DATABASE.md, ARCHITECTURE.md |\n| Provides | ledger, cli.main, cli.testing.cli_runner, cli.errors, cli.validate, cli.money, cli.output, cli.registry |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nProvide the executable and the shared machinery every command uses, so command stories write only\ntheir own behavior.\n\n## Interface\n\n| Module | Contract |\n|---|---|\n| `ledger` | Executable at the application root; runs `cli.main(sys.argv[1:])` and exits with the result. |\n| `cli/__init__.py` | `main(argv: list[str] \\| None = None) -> int`. |\n| `cli/testing.py` | `cli_runner(*args) -> (exit_code, stdout, stderr)` without spawning a process. |\n| `cli/errors.py` | `LedgerError` with an `exit_code` attribute; subclasses `Operational` (1), `Usage` (2), `Rejected` (3), `NotFound` (4). |\n| `cli/registry.py` | `register(path, min_args, max_args, handler)` and `COMMANDS`. A handler is `run(db, args) -> list[list[str]]` returning the header and data rows. |\n| `cli/commands/` | Every module is imported at startup; each registers its handlers. |\n| `cli/database.py` | `open_ledger()` opens the store with `open_database`, using `DATABASE_URL`, then `DATABASE_PATH`, then `data/app.db`. |\n| `cli/validate.py` | `validate_code`, `validate_name`, `validate_memo`, `validate_date`; each returns the value or raises `Rejected`. |\n| `cli/money.py` | `parse_amount(text) -> int` cents and `format_amount(cents) -> str`, integer arithmetic only. |\n| `cli/output.py` | `write_rows(rows)` writes comma-separated rows with `\\n` endings and no quoting; `TRANSACTION_HEADER`, `format_transaction(row)`. |\n\n## Behavior\n\n- The dispatcher resolves the command path and checks the argument count before opening the\n  database. An unknown command, unknown table or report, or wrong argument count exits 2.\n- A `LedgerError` is mapped to its exit code; the message goes to standard error as one line and\n  nothing is written to standard output. Any other database failure exits 1.\n- Output is written only after the handler returns, so a failing command writes nothing to\n  standard output.\n- Codes are 1 to 12 characters from `A-Z` and `0-9`. Names are 1 to 40 characters. Memos are 0 to\n  60 characters. None contains a comma or newline. Dates are real calendar dates `YYYY-MM-DD`.\n- Amounts match `-?DIGITS[.D[D]]`, never zero. Output amounts have exactly two decimals, a leading\n  `-` when negative, and no thousands separator.\n- A non-integer transaction id is rejected with exit 3 (disclosed decision Q-003).\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- None — persistence is owned by DATABASE.md.\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC clifoundation-executable ===\nIntent: The ledger executable exists at the application root and is FEATURE-BalanceRules.md ===\n# FEATURE: Balance Rules\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261025 V1 |\n| Description | Account summaries and the non-negative balance check used by every mutating command. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-CliFoundation.md, DATABASE.md |\n| Provides | cli.balance.account_summary, cli.balance.balances, cli.balance.check_balances, cli.balance.next_txn_id |\n| Consumes | transactions.list, accounts.get |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\nAn account's balance is the sum of its transaction amounts. No command may leave a balance below\nzero. This module computes balances from stored rows in integer cents and applies the rule before a\ncommand writes, so a violation changes nothing.\n\n## Interface\n\n| Function | Contract |\n|---|---|\n| `account_summary(db, code) -> tuple[int, int]` | Transaction count and balance in cents for the account, computed from its stored transactions; `(0, 0)` when it has none. |\n| `balances(db) -> dict[str, int]` | Balance in cents per account that has transactions. |\n| `check_balances(db, deltas)` | `deltas` maps account code to the net change in cents the command would make. Raises `Rejected` (exit 3) when any stored balance plus its delta is below zero; returns `None` otherwise. Writes nothing. |\n| `next_txn_id(db) -> int` | One more than the largest stored `txn_id`, or `1` when the table is empty. |\n\nCommands express a post as `{account: amount}`, an amend as `{account: new - old}`, a delete as\n`{account: -amount}`, a transfer as both sides, and a load as the per-account sum of the file.\nA balance of exactly zero is allowed. Computation is service-side from listed rows (decision Q-002).\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-TRANSACTIONS-003` — read: list transactions for balances and the largest id - supplies: `account` - returns: `txn_id`, `account`, `amount`\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNTS-002` — read: confirm an account exists - supplies: `account` - returns: `account`, `name`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC balancerules-summary ===\nIntent: account_summary returns the stored transaction count and the integer-cent sum of its amounts.\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom cli.balance import account_summary\n\nacct = \"UTBAL1\"\ncat = \"UTBALC1\"\namounts = [1000, -300, 250]\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=acct, name=\"Balance Probe\")\n    db.categories.create(category=cat, description=\"Balance Category\")\n    for offset, cents in enumerate(amounts):\n        db.transactions.create(\n            txn_id=980001 + offset, date=\"2026-04-01\", account=acct,\n            category=cat, amount=cents, memo=\"\",\n        )\n    count, balance = account_summary(db, acct)\n    assert count == len(amounts)\n    assert balance == sum(amounts)\n    assert isinstance(balance, int)\n=== END AC balancerules-summary ===\n\n=== AC balancerules-summary-empty ===\nIntent: An account with no transactions has a zero count and zero Probe\")\n    count, balance = account_summary(db, acct)\n    assert count == 0\n    assert balance == 0\n=== END AC balancerules-summary-empty ===\n\n=== AC balancerules-balances ===\nIntent: balances returns the per-account sums computed from stored rows.\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom cli.balance import balances\n\nacct_a = \"UTBAL3A\"\nacct_b = \"UTBAL3B\"\ncat = \"UTBALC3\"\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=acct_a, name=\"A\")\n    db.accounts.create(account=acct_b, name=\"B\")\n    db.categories.create(category=cat, description=\"Cat\")\n    plan = [(980011, acct_a, 500), (980012, acct_a, -200), (980013, acct_b, 75)]\n    for txn_id, acct, cents in plan:\n        db.transactions.create(\n            txn_id=txn_id, date=\"2026-04-02\", account=acct, category=cat, amount=cents, memo=\"\"\n        )\n    result = balances(db)\n    assert result[acct_a] == sum(c for _, a, c in plan if a == acct_a)\n    assert result[acct_b] == sum(c for _, a, c in plan if a == acct_b)\n=== END AC balancerules-balances ===\n\n=== AC balancerules-check-boundary ===\nIntent: check_balances accepts a change that lands FEATURE-Init.md ===\n# FEATURE: Init\n\n| Field | Value |\n|-------|-------|\n| Version | 20261025 V1 |\n| Description | The ledger init command resets the ledger to empty and writes nothing to standard output. |\n| Depends On | FEATURE-CliFoundation.md, DATABASE.md |\n| Provides | ledger init |\n| Consumes | transactions.list, transactions.delete, categories.list, categories.delete, accounts.list, accounts.delete |\n\n## Questions\n\n- None.\n\n## Purpose\n\n`ledger init` prepares an empty ledger for loading and use and removes existing ledger records when\nrerun. The schema is provisioned by the DATABASE step, so init issues no DDL (decision Q-001).\n\n## Behavior\n\n- Takes no arguments; any argument is a usage error (exit 2).\n- Deletes all transactions first, then all categories, then all accounts, through the declared\n  repository operations, so foreign keys are never violated.\n- Writes nothing to standard output and exits 0. Rerunning leaves an empty ledger.\n\n## Database Interface\n\n- `FEATURE-TRANSACTIONS-003` — read: list transactions to remove - supplies: `account` - returns: `txn_id`\n- `FEATURE-TRANSACTIONS-005` — write: delete each transaction - supplies: `txn_id` - returns: `txn_id`\n- `FEATURE-CATEGORIES-003` — read: list categories to remove - supplies: `category` - returns: `category`\n- `FEATURE-CATEGORIES-004` — write: delete each category - supplies: `category` - returns: `category`\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNTS-003` — read: list accounts to remove - supplies: `account` - returns: `account`\n- `FEATURE-ACCOUNTS-005` — write: delete each account - supplies: `account` - returns: `account`\n\n## Programmatic Acceptance\n\n=== AC init-empties-ledger ===\nIntent: Init removes every account, category, and transaction and exits 0 with no standard output.\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nacct = \"UTINI1\"\ncat = \"UTINIC1\"\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=acct, name=\"Init Probe\")\n    db.categories.create(category=cat, description=\"Init Category\")\n    db.transactions.create(\n        txn_id=970001, date=\"2026-04-01\", account=acct, category=cat, amount=500, memo=\"x\"\n    )\n    code, out, err = cli_runner(\"init\")\n    print(err)\n    assert code == 0\n    assert out == \"\"\n    assert db.transactions.list() == []\n    assert db.categories.list() == []\n    assert db.accounts.list() == []\n=== END AC init-empties-ledger ===\n\n=== AC init-rerun ===\nIntent: Rerunning init on an already empty ledger still exits 0 and leaves it empty.\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nwith db_session() as db:\n    first = cli_runner(\"init\")\n    second = cli_runner(\"init\")\n    assert first[0] == 0\n    assert second[0] == 0\n    assert second[1] == \"\"\n    assert db.accounts.list() == []\n    assert db.categories.list() == []\n    assert db.transactions.list() == []\n=== END AC init-rerun ===\n\n=== AC init-argument-usage ===\nIntent: Init with an extra argument is a usage error that changes nothing.\n\nfrom persistence.testing import db_session\nfrom cli.testing import cli_runner\n\nacct = \"UTINI2\"\nwith db_session() as db:\n    db.accounts.create(account=acct, name=\"Kept\")\n ===\n\n## User Acceptance\n\n- None.\n\n## Guardrails\n\n- Init issues no DDL, drops no table, and does not alter the schema.\n- Init writes nothing to standard output.\n=== END ARTIFACT ===\n===
